### [147] [AuditCoder: Responsibility-Preserving Task Graphs for Auditable Code Generation and Bounded Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29529)
*Kangjie Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AuditCoder是一种将程序与可审计的构建轨迹作为联合输出的代码生成方法，通过合同标注的任务图分配稳定的责任身份，并在验证失败时进行有界修复。在APPS上达到82.5-83.0%的pass@1，在ClassEval上达到75.0-82.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成器只返回最终程序，不保留构建记录，导致无法将失败与产生问题代码的决策相关联，也难以界定合理的修复范围。

Method: 在代码生成前建立合同标注的任务图，为每个承诺分配稳定的责任身份（包含实现、溯源、验证证据和干预历史）。验证失败时，使用保守定位器将异构证据映射到节点或依赖分支（或弃权），只对问题区域进行有界修复，同时复用冻结的互补部分。

Result: APPS上达到82.5-83.0% pass@1，比AgentCoder低7.5-8.5点；ClassEval上达到75.0-82.0%，超过CoT+retry但低于AgentCoder。200份APPS审计记录的任务宏决策-代码轨迹覆盖率为0.9725，定位器在60个失败中识别出26个证据支持的节点或分支，其中17个局部修复通过。

Conclusion: 对于具有稳定、局部可测试边界的任务，任务图不仅作为分解结构，还能作为验证和修复的持久索引发挥作用。AuditCoder恢复了因未修复图分解造成的大部分损失，虽整体性能仍低于AgentCoder，但在可审计性和精准修复方面具有独特价值。

Abstract: Code generators return programs, but typically do not preserve the construction record needed to connect a failure to the decision that produced the affected code or to delimit a justified repair. We present AuditCoder, which treats the program and an auditable construction trace as joint outputs. Before code generation, a contract-annotated task graph assigns stable responsibility identities that remain attached to each commitment, its owned implementation, provenance, validation evidence, and intervention history. When validation fails, a conservative locator maps heterogeneous evidence to a node or dependency branch---or abstains---and bounded repair regenerates only that region while reusing the frozen complement. On APPS, \method{} reaches $82.5$--$83.0\%$ \texttt{pass@1}, recovering much of the loss caused by unrepaired graph decomposition but trailing AgentCoder by $7.5$--$8.5$ points. On ClassEval, it reaches $75.0$--$82.0\%$, outperforming CoT + retry while remaining below AgentCoder. A separate audit of 200 APPS records yields $0.9725$ task-macro decision--code trace coverage; the locator identifies an evidence-supported node or branch for 26 of 60 failures, and 17 of those localized repairs pass. For tasks with stable, locally testable boundaries, the graph functions not only as a decomposition structure but also as a persistent index for validation and repair.

</details>


### [148] [Improving the Understandability of Conceptual Models via Abstract Notation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29552)
*Amine Abbad-Andaloussi,Daniel Jeppe Schütt,Kasper Solhøj Jørgensen,Tobias van Deurs Lundsgaard,Hugo A. López*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"抽象符号工程"（abstract notation engineering）这一语言无关的方法，用于将概念建模中反复出现的低层构型替换为高层次、语义透明的抽象构造，并在DCR图中实例化为DeCleaR扩展，实证验证表明其提升了感知质量、实用质量和用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念模型虽有助于复杂系统的设计、分析和沟通，但当领域级抽象需要通过低层构造来编码（主要出于语义一致性要求）时，模型往往难以理解。已有工作主要改善单个构造的视觉表示，而忽略了反复出现的构型组合这一层面的问题。

Method: 提出抽象符号工程方法，包含四个步骤：模式识别（pattern identification）、模式形式化（pattern formalization）、视觉符号设计（visual notation design）和实证验证（empirical validation）。该方法适用于任意建模语言，并在DCR图中进行了实例化，生成了名为DeCleaR的扩展。

Result: 实证验证结果表明，与标准DCR图相比，DeCleaR在感知经验质量（perceived empirical quality）、实用质量（pragmatic quality）和用户偏好（user preference）方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将关注点从单一构造转移到反复出现的构型组合，并通过抽象符号工程方法以高层抽象替换低层复杂配置，可以有效改善概念模型的可理解性和用户体验。

Abstract: Conceptual modeling supports the design, analysis, and communication of the properties of complex systems, yet conceptual models can be difficult to understand when domain-level abstractions must be encoded through low-level constructs required mainly for semantic conformity. Prior work has mainly improved how existing individual constructs are visually represented. We shift the focus from individual constructs to recurring configurations of constructs, and propose abstract notation engineering as a language-agnostic method for replacing such configurations with higher-level, semantically transparent constructs. The method comprises pattern identification, pattern formalization, visual notation design, and empirical validation. We instantiate it for Dynamic Condition Response (DCR) graphs, where common workflow patterns require elaborate low-level configurations. The resulting extension, DeCleaR, replaces such configurations with compact pattern-based abstractions. The results of our empirical validation show that DeCleaR improves perceived empirical quality, pragmatic quality, and user preference over standard DCR graphs.

</details>


### [149] [Alteron: A Tool for Behavioral Regression Testing Across NLP Classifier Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29557)
*Shazzad Hossain,Proma Chowdhury,Mridha Md. Nafis Fuad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Alteron是一个利用蜕变测试检测NLP模型版本间行为回归的工具，通过10个蜕变关系、4个模型版本和3次模型更新转换的评估，识别出16个行为回归（其中11个为发布阻断级），证明常见模型更新可能在保持总体任务性能的同时引入不良行为变化，而这些变化是传统基准指标无法捕捉的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准指标无法全面捕捉NLP模型跨版本的行为变化，现有工作主要孤立测试模型而非在持续集成流程中比较连续版本，缺乏有效的跨版本行为回归检测手段。

Method: Alteron工具采用蜕变测试方法，从带标签的源样本构建测试语料库，通过对输入进行蜕变变换后比较不同模型版本的输出，从而检测行为回归。

Result: 在10个蜕变关系、4个模型版本和3次模型更新转换的评估中，Alteron识别出16个行为回归，其中11个为发布阻断级别。结果证明常见模型更新可保持总体性能却引入不良行为变化。

Conclusion: 跨模型版本的行为检查能够揭示聚合基准指标无法捕捉的失败，该工具已开源，为NLP模型持续集成中的行为回归检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Evaluating evolving Natural Language Processing (NLP) models is important for ensuring reliable behavior across updates, but standard benchmark metrics do not fully capture how model behavior changes across versions. Existing work has focused mainly on testing models in isolation rather than comparing successive versions in continuous integration workflows. We present Alteron, a tool for detecting behavioral regressions across NLP model versions with metamorphic testing. Alteron constructs a test corpus from labeled source examples and compares model versions on metamorphically transformed inputs. In an evaluation spanning 10 metamorphic relations (MRs), 4 model versions, and 3 model-update transitions, Alteron identified 16 behavioral regressions, 11 of which were release-blocking. The results show that common model updates can preserve overall task performance while still introducing undesirable behavior changes, and that behavioral checks across model versions can reveal failures that aggregate benchmark metrics alone do not capture. The tool is open-source and available at https://github.com/shazzad5709/alteron. A screencast demonstration is available at https://youtu.be/szwiWW5O4do.

</details>


### [150] [Educating the Agentic Engineer: Curricula, Collaboration, and Continuous Learning in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29610)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"代理工程师"这一新职业原型，并引入ACCEL教育框架（包含五大能力支柱和三大交付向量），主张软件工程教育需从"生产人工制品"转向"对自主系统的判断与治理"，通过课程、协作和持续学习三个渠道进行系统性教育改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式和代理式AI正在将软件与系统工程从以人类撰写制品为中心，转变为以指导、验证和治理自主系统为核心。这一范式转变要求培养新型专业人才，但目前缺乏系统的、基于证据的教育架构来支撑这一转变。

Method: 本文采用整合性概念综合方法，跨工程教育、计算教育、人机交互、人因工程和学习科学等多个领域梳理证据，提出了ACCEL框架。该框架包含五大能力支柱，映射到课程、协作和持续学习三个交付向量，并设计了脚手架课程、委托-验证教学循环、重新设计的评估方式及治理素养伦理整合等具体实施方案。

Result: 文章提出了ACCEL框架，明确了五大能力支柱和三重交付向量，设计了委托-验证教学循环以支持人-AI协作，提出了重新设计的评估方案，并使之与现有课程指南和国际AI能力框架对齐。同时识别了自动化偏见、技能退化、浅层参与和问责模糊等关键风险。

Conclusion: 培养代理工程师需要系统性转型而非渐进式课程调整：教学重点必须从生产人工制品转向对日益自主的社会技术系统行使判断力。教育应聚焦于意图规范、多代理工作流编排、机器生成输出批判性评估以及伦理判断。

Abstract: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) are reconfiguring software and systems engineering from a discipline centered on human authorship of artifacts to one focused on directing, verifying, and governing autonomous systems. This transition demands a new professional archetype, the \emph{agentic engineer}, whose enduring value lies in intent specification, orchestration of multi-agent workflows, critical evaluation of machine-generated outputs, and ethical judgment. This article presents an integrative conceptual synthesis across engineering education, computing education, human--AI interaction, human factors, and the learning sciences to derive an evidence-grounded educational architecture for this archetype. We introduce the ACCEL framework (Agentic Competencies through Curricula, Collaboration, and Enduring Learning), which organizes five competency pillars and maps them to three delivery vectors: curricula, collaboration, and continuous learning. Drawing on agency theory, trust-in-automation research, and empirical studies of AI-assisted programming, including evidence that AI benefits are unevenly realized and often misperceived, we propose a scaffolded curriculum, a delegation--verification pedagogical loop for human--AI teaming, redesigned assessment, governance-literate ethics integration, and alignment with current curricular guidelines and international AI competency frameworks. We identify key risks, including automation bias, deskilling, superficial engagement, and diffuse accountability, and conclude that educating the agentic engineer requires systemic transformation rather than incremental curricular change: instruction must shift from producing artifacts to exercising judgment over increasingly autonomous socio-technical systems.

</details>


### [151] [Reusing Past Repairs Through Hierarchical Trajectory Abstraction for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29658)
*Yisen Xu,Jiayuan Zhou,Ruiqi Pan,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STAIR框架将历史修复轨迹转化为多层级可复用计划树，用于引导未来的代码仓库级问题修复。在SWE-bench Verified上，集成Lingxi达到81.2% Pass@1（MiniMax M2.5），生成计划可跨代理泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的修复代理在处理复杂仓库级问题时，将每个问题独立对待，丢弃了以往修复过程中积累的程序性知识，缺乏经验复用机制。

Method: STAIR将历史修复轨迹转换为多级树结构，涵盖从细粒度诊断动作到高层修复策略的多个抽象层级；处理新问题时从不同抽象级别选择相关计划节点，定制为可执行的特定问题计划，并通过提示词提供给代理。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，STAIR集成Lingxi分别达到81.2%和79.2%的Pass@1（分别使用MiniMax M2.5和GPT-5）；计划可跨代理泛化，将mini-SWE-agent v2从75.8%提升至81.0%；消融实验表明混合多抽象级别优于单一级别，未抽象轨迹迁移效果显著较差。

Conclusion: 多层级可复用的修复计划表示能有效捕获跨问题迁移的程序性知识，显著提升代码修复代理的性能，且具备跨代理的泛化能力。

Abstract: Although LLM-driven repair agents can tackle complex, repository-level issues, they treat every issue independently and discard the procedural knowledge accumulated from previous repairs. We introduce STAIR, a framework that converts historical repair trajectories into hierarchical, reusable plans that can be adapted to steer future repairs. Each past trajectory is transformed into a multi-level tree that ranges from fine-grained diagnostic actions to high-level repair strategies, encoding experience at several granularities. When a new issue arrives, STAIR selects relevant plan nodes from multiple abstraction levels, tailors them into executable, issue-specific plans, and supplies them to the agent through its prompt. On SWE-bench Verified, STAIR integrated with Lingxi reaches 81.2% Pass@1 using MiniMax M2.5 and 79.2% using GPT-5. The generated plans also generalize across agents: without any code change, they lift the Pass@1 of a structurally different agent, mini-SWE-agent v2, from 75.8% to 81.0%. Ablation experiments further show that mixing multiple abstraction levels surpasses any single level and that raw, unabstracted trajectories transfer substantially worse.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Bridging the Gap Between PHE and FHE: A Performance and Trade-off Analysis of The Somewhat Homomorphic BGN Cryptosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28700)
*Sefik Serengil,Alper Ozpinar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在lightphe Python框架中集成BGN同态加密方案，将其与PHE（Paillier、Damgard-Jurik、Okamoto-Uchiyama）和FHE（CKKS/TenSEAL）在80/112/128位安全级别下对128维向量操作进行对比。结果表明BGN计算速度较慢（因双线性配对），但公钥大小仅3-6KB（比FHE小5个数量级），支持单次乘法后的无限同态聚合，可用于线性回归推理、余弦相似度等任务，且2位精度即可匹配明文排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGN作为介于PHE和FHE之间的SWHE方案，理论上兼具加法和有限乘法的能力，但缺乏易于使用的软件实现，阻碍了其在实际隐私计算场景中的采用。

Method: 将BGN集成到lightphe Python框架中，提供几行代码即可部署的API；在多安全级别下对加密128维向量操作进行基准测试，并与四种方案对比，评估计算性能、通信开销和解密精度。

Result: BGN在计算速度上因双线性配对运算而慢于PHE和SIMD优化的FHE，但公钥大小仅3-6KB，比FHE小多达5个数量级。BGN支持单次乘法后无限的蜜态聚合，且采用2位优化精度即可匹配明文排序基线，克服了目标群离散对数解密瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过开源lightphe集成，BGN被确立为面向带宽受限、去中心化架构的实用同态加密引擎，在带宽效率和功能完整性之间提供了独特的平衡点。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving data analytics, but practitioners often face a trade-off between lightweight Partially Homomorphic Encryption (PHE) and computationally dominant Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE). The Boneh-Goh-Nissim (BGN) cryptosystem bridges this gap as a Somewhat Homomorphic Encryption (SWHE) scheme supporting unlimited additions and one ciphertext multiplication. Despite its algebraic elegance, practical BGN adoption has been hindered by a lack of accessible software implementations. This paper presents a comparative analysis of BGN against PHE and FHE paradigms through its integration into the lightphe Python framework, allowing deployment in just a few lines of code. We benchmark encrypted 128-dimensional vector operations under 80-bit, 112-bit and 128-bit security levels against Paillier, Damgard-Jurik, Okamoto-Uchiyama, and the FHE CKKS scheme via TenSEAL. Results reveal a computation-communication trade-off: BGN is computationally slower due to bilinear pairings compared to PHE and SIMD-optimized FHE, but retains a microscopic public key size of 3-6 KB, up to five orders of magnitude smaller than FHE. Crucially, BGN enables boundless homomorphic aggregation after a single multiplication, supporting complex tasks such as linear regression inference, Cosine Similarity, and Squared Euclidean Distance. Furthermore, an optimized precision of 2 digits suffices to match plaintext ranking baselines, overcoming the target-group discrete logarithm decryption bottleneck. By open-sourcing this pipeline in lightphe, this work establishes BGN as a practical engine for bandwidth-constrained, decentralized architectures.

</details>


### [153] [Costs of Arbitrary Real Matrix Factorizations for Pure-DP Continual Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28703)
*Awnon Bhowmik,Mahmudul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了纯ε-差分隐私下Laplace矩阵机制的因子化成本，证明了对下三角前缀和矩阵T_n，其均方误差和最大逐坐标均方误差的因子化成本均为Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，无需符号、稀疏性或方阵限制。由此得到优化后的均方误差和最大均方误差均为Θ(ε^{-2}log³(n+1))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纯ε-差分隐私的Laplace矩阵机制中，因子化成本是决定平方误差上界的关键量。此前Arkhipov和Kalinin仅在{0,1}因子条件下证明了匹配下界，并将任意实数因子的推广列为开放问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 通过p-核范数障碍建立聚合列宽估计D_k(T_n)≍n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}；2. 利用Pietsch和Hinrichs-Pietsch的经典近似空间转换，在临界指数p=2/3处达到调和；3. 借助Hölder不等式将下界传递至两个因子化成本；4. 通过Fenwick区间因子化构造匹配的上界。

Result: 证明了c_frob(T_n)和c_two(T_n)均为Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，同时完整刻画了0<p<1范围内nucpow_p(T_n)的阶：p<2/3时为n，p=2/3时为n log n，p>2/3时为n^{3p/2}。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了纯ε-DP下矩阵机制的优化均方误差和最大均方误差均为Θ(ε^{-2}log³(n+1))，解决了开放问题。但结论仅限纯ε-DP Laplace矩阵机制及两种平方误差准则，不涵盖非矩阵持续机制、近似DP灵敏度或坐标间期望最大值。

Abstract: Let \(T_n\) be the lower-triangular prefix-sum matrix and let \(\cfrob(T_n)\) and \(\ctwo(T_n)\) be the factorization costs that govern mean and maximum per-coordinate squared error of the Laplace matrix mechanism under pure \(\eps\)-differential privacy, for \(\eps>0\). We prove \(\cfrob(T_n),\ctwo(T_n)=Θ\bigl((\log(n+1))^{3/2}\bigr)\) with no sign, sparsity, or squareness restriction and with arbitrary finite inner dimension. Consequently, within the pure-\(\eps\)-DP matrix-mechanism class the optimized maximum and mean squared errors are both \(Θ(\eps^{-2}\log^{3}(n+1))\). Under the factorization contract of Arkhipov and Kalinin (arXiv:2607.08963v1), who prove the matching lower order for factors with entries in \(\{0,1\}\) and state the arbitrary-factor extension as open, the theorem below establishes the order for arbitrary real factors. The lower bound runs through a \(p\)-nuclear obstruction: an aggregate column-width estimate \(D_k(T_n)\asymp n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}\), valid in the low-rank range \(1\leq k\leq n/16\), for the prefix chain, fed into the classical approximation-space conversion of Pietsch and Hinrichs--Pietsch, becomes harmonic at the critical exponent \(p=2/3\), and Hölder's inequality transfers it to both factorization costs. The same computation determines \(\nucpow_p(T_n)\) for each fixed \(0<p<1\): order \(n\) below \(2/3\), \(n\log n\) at \(2/3\), and \(n^{3p/2}\) above. A Fenwick interval factorization supplies matching upper bounds. The claims are confined to pure-\(\eps\)-DP Laplace matrix mechanisms and the two stated squared-error criteria; they do not cover non-matrix continual mechanisms, approximate-DP sensitivity, or expected maxima across coordinates.

</details>


### [154] [Blockchain Transaction Simulation Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28747)
*Xiaocan Wang,Shixuan Guan,Tong Yang,Xiapu Luo,Yue Duan,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了"交易模拟钓鱼"攻击，该攻击利用依赖动态区块链状态的精心设计的智能合约，使钱包的交易模拟功能显示良性或盈利结果，但实际链上执行却将用户资金重定向至攻击者地址。作者提出了SIMGUARD，一个基于字节码的检测系统，在四条链上检测到超过4000个钓鱼合约，涉及超过5700名受害者和约348万美元损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流加密钱包（如MetaMask）引入的交易模拟功能虽然能防御传统的资金抽取攻击，但其本身可以被新型钓鱼技术利用。攻击者利用交易模拟与实际链上执行之间的差异来欺骗用户，这一安全漏洞此前未被系统研究，迫切需要填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出SIMGUARD，一种字节码级别的检测系统，结合静态分析和动态程序分析来识别钓鱼合约。研究首先构建了可用于实施该攻击的钓鱼合约分类体系，然后在以太坊、币安智能链、Avalanche和Polygon四条区块链上进行大规模检测和聚类分析。

Result: 在2024年8月至2025年6月期间，检测到超过4000个钓鱼合约，识别出超过5700名受害者和约348万美元的损失，其中91.5%的损失发生在以太坊上。聚类分析显示最大的钓鱼合约集群占总损失的约83%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前钱包防御机制的关键弱点，强调了开发更稳健的交易模拟机制的紧迫性。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency users have increasingly become targets of phishing and scam attacks. To mitigate these threats, leading crypto wallets (e.g., MetaMask) have introduced transaction simulation, which previews a transaction's balance changes before on-chain execution. While effective against traditional fund-draining attacks, we show that this defense can itself be exploited by a new phishing technique, which we term transaction simulation phishing. This attack uses carefully crafted smart contracts whose execution depends on dynamic blockchain state, causing simulations to display benign or profitable outcomes while the actual on-chain execution redirects users' funds to attacker-controlled addresses.
  We present the first comprehensive study of transaction simulation phishing. We first develop a taxonomy of phishing contracts that can be utilized to facilitate this attack. Then, we propose SIMGUARD, a bytecode-level detection system that combines static and dynamic program analysis to identify phishing contracts. Applying SIMGUARD to Ethereum, Binance Smart Chain, Avalanche, and Polygon, we detect over 4,000 phishing contracts deployed between August 2024 and June 2025. Our analysis identifies more than 5,700 victims and approximately $3.48 million USD in losses, 91.5% of which occurred on Ethereum. Moreover, our clustering result reveals that the largest phishing contract cluster alone accounts for about 83% of the total losses. These results expose a critical weakness in current wallet defenses and highlight the urgent need for more robust transaction simulation mechanisms.

</details>


### [155] [Partial Derandomization for Leakage-Resilient Shamir's Secret Sharing over Composite Order Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28757)
*S. Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对复合阶域上抗泄漏Shamir秘密共享方案的显式求值位置构造问题取得进展。基于Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025)的二分法结果，提出了一种部分去随机化方法：用简单固定有理函数Φ的迭代序列替代n个独立随机求值位置，将随机性从nd log p比特降至d log p比特，在n = O(d/log_p d)且重构阈值k≥2的参数范围内，实现单块泄漏下的完美安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maji等人(EUROCRYPT 2024)证明了随机求值位置能构造统计安全、抗物理比特泄漏的Shamir秘密共享，但随机性开销较大（nd log p比特）。Nguyen(EUROCRYPT 2025)的二分法表明线性码秘密共享方案要么统计安全要么完全不安全。作者希望减少随机性消耗，实现求值位置的部分去随机化。

Method: 作者利用有理函数Φ的迭代序列x_j = Φ^j(x_0)替换n个独立随机求值位置，其中初始点x_0随机选择。关键技术是部分分式非退化（partial fraction nondegeneracy）论证，利用有理迭代的不同极点来保证安全性。构造适用于n = O(d/log_p d)且k≥2的参数范围。

Result: 随机性从nd log p比特降至d log p比特，且在n = O(d/log_p d)和任意重构阈值k≥2的参数范围内，该方案在单块泄漏下达到完美安全（统计距离恰好为零），优于Maji等人的统计安全结果。

Conclusion: 通过有理函数迭代的部分去随机化，在特定参数范围内显著降低了泄漏鲁棒Shamir秘密共享方案的随机性需求，并实现了比此前更强的完美安全性保证。该方法为复合阶域上抗泄漏秘密共享的显式构造提供了新思路。

Abstract: We make progress on the question of constructing explicit evaluation places for leakage-resilient Shamir's secret sharing, over composite order fields. Previously, Maji et al. (EUROCRYPT 2024) showed that random evaluation places yield Shamir's secret sharing over the composite order field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ that is statistically secure against physical-bit leakage. Later, Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025) established a dichotomy that linear code-based secret-sharing scheme over the field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ is either statistically secure or completely insecure against such leakage.
  Building upon Nguyen's dichotomy, we present a partial derandomization of evaluation places, improving upon the Maji et al. result for a restricted regime of parameters. We replace the random choice of $n$ independent evaluation places by the iterates $x_j = Φ^j(x_0)$ of a simple fixed rational function $Φ$, where the initial point $x_0 \in \mathbb{F}_{p^d}^*$ is randomly chosen. The randomness in the evaluation places thus drops from $nd \log p$ bits to $d\log p$ bits. Our construction is valid for the regime $n = O(d/\log_p d)$, and any reconstruction threshold $k \ge 2$; in fact, the scheme attains perfect security (statistical distance exactly zero) against single-block leakage. Our technique is a partial fraction nondegeneracy argument that exploits the distinct poles of the rational iterates.

</details>


### [156] [YazSes: An Offline, Privacy-First, Cross-Platform Hold-to-Talk Voice-Dictation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28878)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: YazSes是一个完全在设备端运行的、开源的语音听写守护进程，支持Linux、macOS和Windows，通过faster-whisper实现本地语音转文字，配备正则命令语法和可选的小语言模型路由器实现编辑器/终端操作映射，所有数据均不出设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端语音听写服务准确率高但需将语音流式传输到远程服务器，在隐私敏感行业和离线/气隙环境中不可接受；现有本地替代方案要么受平台限制，要么面向专家脚本编写而非即插即用的听写场景。

Method: 采用协议式平台抽象层，单一代码库支持三大桌面平台；使用faster-whisper（CPU、int8量化）进行本地转录；通过快速正则命令语法和可选小语言模型路由器实现动作映射；记录采用按住说话模式而非持续监听；可选的个性化循环在设备端加密维护语料库，仅提出配置更改建议而不外传数据；JSON-RPC控制面管理分层流水线架构。

Result: 在200条LibriSpeech测试集上，词错误率从tiny.en的4.82%到small.en的2.59%，实时因子0.520（CPU上无需GPU即快于实时）；命令语法达到100%动作准确率和0%误报率（纯听写场景），每次调用仅0.021ms，非解码流水线开销为0.289ms。macOS和Windows后端已实现并通过单元测试，但未做端到端评估。

Conclusion: YazSes证明了在商用CPU上实现完全本地化、跨平台、隐私安全的语音听写与命令控制是可行的，同时保持了与云端服务相当的准确率和优于实时的解码速度，系统及可复现的基准测试工具均已开源。

Abstract: Cloud voice-dictation services deliver strong accuracy but require streaming a user's speech to a remote provider, an unacceptable trade-off in privacy-sensitive professions and offline or air-gapped settings; the leading on-device alternatives are either platform-locked or aimed at expert scripting rather than plug-and-play dictation. We present YazSes, an open-source (Apache-2.0) hold-to-talk voice dictation daemon that runs entirely on-device, with a single codebase targeting Linux, macOS, and Windows through a protocol-based platform abstraction. YazSes transcribes speech locally with faster-whisper (CPU, int8) and injects the result into the focused application; a fast regex command grammar, backed by an optional small-language-model router, maps utterances to editor and terminal actions. Nothing leaves the machine: recording is push-to-talk rather than always-listening, there is no telemetry, and an opt-in personalization loop keeps its corpus encrypted on-device and proposes configuration changes instead of shipping data out. We describe the system architecture -- a staged pipeline behind a protocol-based platform abstraction with a JSON-RPC control plane -- and its privacy and threat model. We evaluate the shipping Python implementation on a single commodity Linux laptop; the macOS and Windows backends are implemented and unit-tested but not end-to-end evaluated here. On 200 LibriSpeech test-clean utterances spanning 40 speakers, word error rate ranges from 4.82% (tiny.en) to 2.59% (small.en) at a real-time factor of 0.520 for small.en, decoding faster than real time on CPU with no GPU. The command grammar reaches 100% action accuracy with a 0.0% false-positive rate on plain dictation at 0.021 ms per call, and the non-decode pipeline adds 0.289 ms of overhead. The system and the reproducible benchmark harness behind every number in this paper are public.

</details>


### [157] [Hollow-LLM Attack: Computationally Trivial Weights in Zero-Knowledge Verification of LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28884)
*Chen Gong,Beijie Liu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零知识（ZK）大语言模型推理验证的一个关键盲区：现有ZK验证仅保证输出与公开架构及承诺权重的等式一致性，但无法约束提供者实际付出的计算量。作者形式化了这一"努力差距"，并提出Hollow-LLM攻击，使不诚实的提供者通过嵌入"幽灵权重"（ghost weights）在保持声明架构和参数数量的同时，将有效计算塌缩为远小于声明模型规模的计算，且仍能通过验证电路生成有效证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型规模不断扩大且主要通过远程平台提供服务，验证推理执行的忠实性（确保提供者实际执行声明的模型而非篡改或缩小版本）变得至关重要。ZK推理承诺提供公开可验证性和逐实例的等式正确性保证，但现有方案无法约束提供者为生成输出所投入的计算量，存在被利用的经济激励漏洞。

Method: 作者首先形式化了ZK推理验证中的"努力差距"（effort gap），即验证电路不绑定实际计算工作量。然后提出Hollow-LLM攻击框架：设计具体的幽灵权重族，使其与标准Transformer模块兼容，通过代数结构塌缩有效计算，同时保持声明的架构和参数数量。这些幽灵权重能够满足验证电路约束并产生有效证明。

Result: 作者刻画了与标准Transformer模块兼容的具体幽灵权重族，并证明这种"空心"部署在相同验证电路下能够显著降低服务成本且零质量损失。这形成了一个有利可图的经济均衡：不诚实的提供者可以以小型模型的计算成本交付"可证明正确"的输出，同时虚报模型规模。

Conclusion: 研究揭示证明推理正确性不等同于证明大规模模型执行，现有ZK验证体系需要额外的保护机制来将正确性与可验证的计算工作量绑定。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in scale and are predominantly served from remote platforms, verifying faithful inference execution becomes critical (i.e., ensuring that a provider actually executes the advertised model and computational workload rather than a tampered or downsized variant). Zero-knowledge (ZK) LLM inference offers an appealing approach. It promises public verifiability and delivers per-instance guarantees of equational correctness by proving that an output is consistent with executing a public architecture under committed, private weights. Though, we show that it does not bind the effort expended to produce the output. In this paper, we formalize this overlooked effort gap and introduce the Hollow-LLM Attack, in which a dishonest provider retains the declared architecture and parameter count but embeds ghost weights whose algebraic structure collapses effective computation. These witnesses satisfy the verification circuit and yield valid proofs, even though the dishonest model owner, who serves as the prover, performs computation commensurate with a much smaller model than the declared public architecture. This creates a profitable equilibrium in which providers deliver provably correct outputs at small-model cost while overclaiming model size. Accordingly, we characterize concrete families of ghost weights that compose with standard transformer blocks and show that such hollow deployments substantially reduce serving cost with zero quality loss under the same verification circuit. These findings underscore that proof of correct inference is not proof of large-model execution and necessitate additional protections to bind correctness to verifiable computational work.

</details>


### [158] [A Biometric Sensor Network to Enable Real-Time Measurement of Individual Student Engagement in STEM Lecture Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28944)
*Ahmed Elsayed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生物传感器网络（BSN），用于在STEM课堂中实时、无侵入地测量和持续追踪学生个体参与度，通过分布式学生处理单元（SPUs）实现设备端处理，兼顾隐私保护和实时分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生参与度是STEM教育中学业表现和保留率的关键预测指标，但现有测量方法往往具有侵入性、依赖人工操作或无法满足课堂实时应用需求。

Method: 提出由学生处理单元（SPUs）组成的生物传感器网络（BSN），每个SPU支持数据集收集模式（构建私有SE数据集）和分析模式（对10秒视频段进行设备端实时推理）。系统集成摄像头感测、计算机视觉流水线（人脸检测、视线估计、情感分析）、无线网络、安全协议和会话级数据管理，确保非侵入性、非侵犯性、非污名化、实时性和自动化五个目标。

Result: 论文构建了完整的BSN系统架构，涵盖硬件设计、视觉处理、无线组网、安全协议和数据管理，实现从行为、情感和认知三个维度捕捉学生参与度指标，且原始视频数据不离开设备。

Conclusion: 该BSN系统为STEM课堂学生参与度提供了符合伦理和隐私约束的实时自动测量方案，兼具非侵入性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Student engagement (SE) is a critical predictor of academic performance and retention in STEM education, yet existing measurement approaches are often intrusive, manually intensive, or unsuitable for real-time classroom use. This thesis proposes a novel $\textit{Biometric Sensor Network}$ (BSN) designed to enable real-time measurement and continuous tracking of individual student engagement in STEM classroom environments. The system enables capturing of behavioral, emotional, and cognitive indicators through camera-based sensing while preserving ethical and privacy constraints.
  To measure these indicators unobtrusively and ethically, we propose a BSN composed of $\textit{Student Processing Units}$ (SPUs) that function as distributed sensing nodes. The network is explicitly designed to satisfy five objectives: it must be $\textbf{non-intrusive}, \textbf{non-invasive}, \textbf{non-stigmatizing}, \textbf{real-time}$, and $\textbf{automatic}$, while ensuring rigorous protection of student data security and privacy.
  Each SPU supports two operational modes: (i) a $\textit{dataset-collection mode}$, in which raw student video is temporarily recorded to construct a private SE dataset for model training and validation, and (ii) an $\textit{analysis mode}$, in which the SPU performs real-time inference on 10-second video segments without storing or transmitting raw frames. In this analysis role, each SPU enables fully on-device processing---including face detection, gaze estimation, and affective analysis---ensuring that no identifiable video data leaves the device. A secure backend infrastructure manages device authentication, session orchestration, and encrypted data ingestion. The full system integrates hardware design, computer-vision pipelines, wireless networking, security protocols, and session-level data management.

</details>


### [159] [MESS: Fast and Private Semantic Search on Multi-Graph HNSW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28999)
*Haoyu Cui,Zengpeng Li,Tien Tuan Anh Dinh,Mei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MESS系统，通过将向量映射为二进制码、应用LSH和随机响应扰动，并构建多图HNSW索引，实现了语义搜索的隐私性、准确性和高效性三者兼顾，相比最先进基线延迟降低最多15.08倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义搜索系统在可信云提供者场景下无法保护数据和查询隐私。已有方案（同态加密HE、ORAM、差分隐私DP）都未能同时满足隐私、准确性和效率三个属性，缺乏三者兼顾的解决方案。

Method: MESS将原始向量映射为二进制码，应用局部敏感哈希（LSH）和随机响应进行扰动，并在扰动码上构建多图分层可导航小世界（HNSW）索引。通过两阶段查询扰动机制确保搜索模式隐私，多图索引缓解扰动对结果质量的影响。

Result: MESS在隐私方面提供了正式的安全分析，在性能方面经广泛评估，达到比最先进基线低最多15.08倍的延迟。

Conclusion: MESS成功实现了隐私、准确性和效率三属性的统一，证明在不使用同态加密或ORAM的情况下，通过编码和扰动结合多图索引即可构建高效且隐私保护的语义搜索系统。

Abstract: Semantic search systems map data to a high-dimensional vector space and support retrieval of similar data via approximate nearest neighbor search. When the system is hosted by a trusted cloud provider, there is no privacy for the data or the query. Our goal is to design a system with three properties: privacy, accuracy, and efficiency. Existing works adopt either homomorphic encryption (HE), oblivious RAM (ORAM), or a differential privacy (DP) approach. They fall short of achieving all three properties.
  In this paper, we present MESS, a system that realizes our goal. It maps the original vectors into binary codes, applies locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) and randomized response, and constructs a multi-graph Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) index over the perturbed codes. MESS ensures data, query, and access pattern privacy. It also ensures search pattern privacy via a two-phase query perturbation mechanism. The multi-graph index mitigates the impact of perturbation on result quality, thereby achieving accuracy. MESS is efficient because search is performed directly over perturbed codes, without the overhead of homomorphic encryption or ORAM. We give formal analysis of the system's privacy and extensive evaluation of its performance. The results show that MESS achieves up to 15.08\times lower latency than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [160] [GoldenRetriever: Non-Interactive Homomorphic Encrypted Retrieval for Privacy-Preserving RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29019)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Dongdong Wang,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架用于RAG系统，利用CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度评估和文档选择，将计算复杂度从二次降至线性，并通过精度稳定的掩码极化方法实现文档的准确恢复，在保证检索效果的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG管道在明文数据上运行，存在隐私泄露风险。尽管已有基于同态加密和私有信息检索的隐私保护方案，但它们依赖交互式协议或基于排序的选择机制，导致高延迟和潜在信息泄露。因此需要一个高效且非交互式的加密检索框架。

Method: 提出基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架：1) 采用阈值选择代替top-k排序，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)；2) 使用CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度计算和文档选择，不泄露查询内容、中间分数和选中索引；3) 引入精度稳定的掩码极化方法来弥合近似加密计算与离散令牌重建之间的差距。

Result: 在标准检索基准上的实验表明，该方法在实现有竞争力的检索效果的同时，相比基于排序的加密方法显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 阈值选择机制可作为可扩展且安全的RAG系统的实用基础，在隐私保护和检索效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but existing pipelines typically operate on plaintext data, raising significant privacy concerns. Prior work on privacy-preserving retrieval leverages cryptographic techniques such as homomorphic encryption (HE) and private information retrieval (PIR), but often relies on interactive protocols or ranking-based selection mechanisms that incur high latency and potential information leakage. In this paper, we propose a practical non-interactive encrypted retrieval framework for RAG based on threshold selection. Instead of performing expensive top-$k$ ranking under encryption, our approach selects documents whose similarity scores exceed a predefined threshold, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to linear in the corpus size. We implement this design using CKKS-based homomorphic computation, enabling fully encrypted similarity evaluation and document selection without revealing query content, intermediate scores, or selected indices. To bridge the gap between approximate encrypted computation and discrete token reconstruction, we introduce a precision-stable mask polarization method that ensures accurate recovery of selected documents. Experiments on standard retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive retrieval effectiveness while significantly reducing latency compared to ranking-based encrypted methods. These results highlight threshold-based selection as a practical foundation for scalable and secure RAG systems.

</details>


### [161] [Memory Provenance Laundering in LLM Agents: A Non-Amplification Firewall for Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29167)
*Jinghan Xu,Yiyong Xiao,Wanru Shao,Hankai Liu,Xinjin Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"记忆来源洗白"的攻击：LLM智能体记忆整合过程中，外部低信任观察可能被改写为可信的用户历史或工作流支持，从而保留动作触发条件却抹去来源权威性。作者形式化该边界并设计PPMF（来源保留记忆防火墙）中间件，通过保留平台维护的来源信息并依据动作风险匹配记忆权威性来授权工具调用。实验表明，在固定风险策略下，未经保护的脆弱记忆攻击成功率可达100%，而PPMF能拦截所有未授权高风险动作，同时保持正常操作可执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的长期记忆虽能复用偏好与工作流，但会把不可信观察变成持久动作上下文，造成"记忆来源洗白"安全问题——低信任来源在丢失记忆整合后可能被伪装为高信任来源，而现有提示过滤器、内容清理器和工具防护均无法在损失性记忆整合后强制执行来源权威性不放大的边界。

Method: 形式化"来源权威性不放大"的安全边界，设计PPMF——一种轻量级记忆中间件，保留平台维护的记忆来源元数据，并根据动作风险等级匹配动作相关记忆的权威性来授权工具调用，实现来源追踪与风险门控的结合。

Result: 基于schema的评估显示：存在漏洞的整合记忆攻击成功率最高达100%；而在保留平台来源信息、确认机制和风险标签后，没有任何未授权高风险动作能通过PPMF门控，同时已确认的正常动作和低风险记忆使用仍可正常执行。

Conclusion: PPMF通过保存来源元数据和风险感知授权，在不牺牲正常功能的前提下有效阻断基于来源洗白的攻击，为LLM智能体记忆系统提供了实用的安全防护边界。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets large language model(LLM) agents reuse prior preferences and work flows, but it also turns untrusted observations into persistent action context. We identify memory provenance laundering: during LLM-based memory consolidation, an external observation may be rewritten as apparent user history or workflow support, preserving an action trigger while erasing the low-trust source that should limit its authority. Existing prompt filters, content sanitizers, and tool guards do not enforce source-authority non-amplification after lossy memory consolidation. We formalize this boundary and instantiate it as Provenance-Preserving Memory Fire wall (PPMF), a lightweight memory middleware that preserves platform-maintained provenance and authorizes tool calls by matching action risk to the authority of action-relevant memories. In our schema-grounded evaluation with fixed risk policies, vulnerable consolidated memories reach up to 1.000 attack success rate(ASR); with intact platform-maintained provenance, confirmation, and risk labels, no evaluated unauthorized high-risk action passes the PPMF gate while confirmed benign actions and targeted low-risk memory use remain executable.

</details>


### [162] [Alignment Is Local: A Paired Diagnostic for GUI Agents under User-Side Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29199)
*Haoxin An,Yunpeng Song,Zihao Bai,Zhongmin Cai,Guojun Xiong,Chenhao Lin,Wentao Chen,Feng Wei,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，提示级别对齐（如单行护栏）在移动GUI代理中仅对单轮、明确表达的恶意请求有效，在多轮对话链和隐蔽请求等真实场景中防御效果显著退化，静态单轮评估会系统性高估实际部署的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动代理主要依赖提示级对齐作为轻量防御手段，但这种防御是否能在动态交互和真实威胁条件下保持可靠尚不清楚——单轮拒绝显式恶意请求并不能代表真实部署中的安全性。

Method: 采用配对诊断方法，在三个前沿GUI代理上测试屏幕级用户侧说服（无环境注入），对比单轮独立探测与四轮升级链攻击，同时考察显式与隐蔽请求两种场景，并量化护栏的过度拒绝成本与有效攻击成功率（ASR）。

Result: 单行护栏在单轮场景下可将ASR降低约40个百分点且几乎无过度拒绝成本；但多轮升级链使受保护ASR回升约20个百分点。护栏侵蚀在不同模型上表现不同：Qwen面临实质性的护栏退化，而Claude和GPT的风险与防御基本正交。护栏存在时隐蔽请求的成功率反超显式请求，说明防御主要在意图被明确表述时才会生效。

Conclusion: 静态单轮ASR会以系统性和可预测的方式高估部署后的实际鲁棒性，提示级对齐是局部现象而非全局保障，需要更全面的多轮与隐蔽场景评估框架来验证GUI代理的可信部署。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of GUI agents in ubiquitous computing settings requires alignment that survives dynamic interaction and precise threat conditions, not just single-turn refusal of explicit harmful requests. We argue that prompt-level alignment, the dominant lightweight defense in current mobile agents, is a local phenomenon: it works reliably only in the narrow evaluation slice where it is typically measured, namely single-turn, explicitly-verbalized intent, and degrades systematically along two axes that any real user can traverse. Using a paired diagnostic on three frontier GUI agents, screen-grounded, user-side persuasion, with no environment injection, we show that a one-line guardrail achieves large single-shot ASR reductions, up to roughly 40 points, at near-zero over-refusal cost. Nevertheless, moving from independent probes to four-turn escalation chains raises guarded ASR by approximately 20 points on every model. Relative to the neutral baselines, this increase reflects substantial guardrail erosion for Qwen but a largely defense-orthogonal dynamic risk for Claude and GPT. The sign of the salience gap flips under the guardrail: concealed requests are not systematically more successful than explicit ones without a guardrail, but are more successful with one, indicating that the defense engages primarily when intent is named. Static single-turn ASR therefore overstates deployed robustness by a systematic and predictable margin.

</details>


### [163] [MOSAIC: Masked Outsourcing of Secure AI Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29221)
*James Hsin-yu Chiang,Sheila Zingg,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种新型矩阵乘法掩码协议，使计算能力弱的客户端能够安全地将大规模AI计算（如70B参数Transformer推理）外包给不可信服务器，同时保护客户端持有的输入和模型。通过引入少量噪声放松正确性约束，实现了最优的渐进客户端开销和比先前工作快数个数量级的实际运行时间，其安全性基于decisional LWE和LPN假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全外包AI计算方案受限于矩阵规模，无法支撑现代大规模Transformer推理等实际负载，且客户端开销过高。作者旨在解决客户端持有输入和模型、服务器不得知悉二者的场景下，安全且高效地外包大规模AI计算的问题。

Method: MOSAIC的核心是新型矩阵乘法掩码协议，通过向乘法结果引入少量噪声来放宽正确性要求，实现最优的渐进客户端开销。针对噪声在transformer多层中累积的问题，提出基于随机Hadamard旋转的误差缩放机制来约束误差增长。安全性归约到decisional LWE和LPN假设。

Result: 在70B参数Transformer模型上，MOSAIC的困惑度与主流量化方法相当，在HumanEval基准上甚至匹配全精度BF16推理。实际运行时间比先前工作快数个数量级，并展示了在采用RDMA类网络、按阶段/层/时间分布推理的现代数据中心中的端到端实现。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过保持较小的可信计算基（TCB），将大量AI计算外包给不可信加速器，为大规模机密AI计算在数据中心中的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We address the challenge of securely and efficiently outsourcing AI computations from a trusted but computationally weak client to an untrusted but powerful server, in the setting where the client holds both the input and the model, and the server must learn neither. We present MOSAIC, whose core is a novel matrix-multiplication masking protocol that scales to far larger matrices than prior work, enabling the safe outsourcing of modern workloads such as large transformer inference. By introducing small amounts of noise to the multiplication result and thereby relaxing correctness, MOSAIC achieves optimal asymptotic client overhead and concrete runtimes orders of magnitude faster than prior work. Its security reduces to the decisional LWE and LPN assumptions.
  Because this noise accumulates across the many layers of a transformer, a key technical challenge is bounding error growth; MOSAIC addresses this with an error-scaling mechanism based on random Hadamard rotations. On large 70B transformer models, MOSAIC's perplexity is comparable to popular quantization approaches and even matches full-precision BF16 inference on HumanEval.
  Finally, we present an end-to-end implementation showing how ideas like MOSAIC can promise a path towards large-scale confidential AI in modern data centers. Non-confidential inference is already distributed across phase (prefill/decode), layer, and time to maximize utilization of heterogeneous hardware, using RDMA-like networking to move activations, cached KV values, and weights across nodes. MOSAIC enables scaling of confidential compute by keeping the trusted computing base (TCB) small and outsourcing the bulk of the AI computation to untrusted accelerators.

</details>


### [164] [JUNO: Aggregated Vector Consensus for Optimal Asynchronous Common Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29244)
*Liangrong Zhao,Qin Wang,Joseph K. Liu,Jiangshan Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的异步网络下的向量共识原语——聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus），并基于此实现了Juno协议，以最优O(n²)消息复杂度实现异步公共子集（ACS）。实验表明，与HoneyBadgerBFT和Dory相比，Juno分别实现了93%和47%的平均吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量共识协议在完全异步网络中的应用存在空白，作者希望填补这一缺口，设计一种能够在异步网络环境下高效实现的向量共识原语。

Method: 提出聚合向量共识原语作为新的共识基元，通过输出聚合自各进程输入的向量来实现一致性；基于该原语设计Juno协议，实现异步公共子集（ACS）并达到最优的O(n²)消息复杂度。

Result: 实验评估表明，Juno相比HoneyBadgerBFT平均吞吐量提升93%，相比Dory提升47%，验证了其在异步网络中的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功填补了向量共识协议在完全异步网络中应用的空白，所提出的Juno协议在消息复杂度和实际吞吐性能上均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose \textit{aggregated vector consensus}, a new vector consensus primitive designed for asynchronous networks. The primitive achieves agreement by outputting a vector of values aggregated from independent process inputs. We then introduce \textsc{Juno}, an asynchronous common subset (ACS) protocol that fully implements our aggregated vector consensus to attain optimal $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ message complexity.
  We further implement and evaluate \textsc{Juno} in comparison with the legacy HoneyBadgerBFT and the state-of-the-art Dory. Experiment results demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our protocol demonstrates an average throughput performance improvement of 93\% compared with HoneyBadgerBFT and a 47\% improvement compared with Dory. Notably, our study makes significant progress in addressing the gap in applying vector consensus protocol in fully asynchronous networks.

</details>


### [165] [On the Resilience of 5G NR Against Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29384)
*Sotiris Michaelides,Fidelius Lula,Daniel Eguiguren Chavez,Michael Rademacher,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在ns-3模拟器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰器，系统地比较了不同维度（如蜂窝技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽）下5G网络对蜂窝干扰（jamming）的韧性表现。结果表明，蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性影响不大，而信道带宽和频率范围是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G网络在工业网络和关键基础设施等可用性关键系统中的广泛应用，全面理解其对蜂窝干扰的韧性至关重要。然而现有研究仅针对固定5G物理层配置进行孤立评估，难以进行合理比较（如不同频段或信道带宽在干扰韧性方面的差异），缺乏系统性的对比框架。

Method: 在ns-3模拟器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰器，利用模拟的可控确定性，从多个维度（蜂窝技术、频率、子载波间隔、信道带宽）系统比较蜂窝干扰对5G网络的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示，蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性影响甚微，而信道带宽和频率范围在提升干扰韧性方面起到关键作用。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了5G网络干扰韧性系统性比较研究的空白，提供了开源工具，为5G网络的抗干扰设计和部署提供了重要参考，指出了信道带宽和频率范围是提升抗干扰能力的核心因素。

Abstract: With the increasing use of 5G networks in availability-critical systems, including industrial networks and critical infrastructure, a comprehensive understanding of their resilience to cellular jamming has become imperative. However, research so far has focused on isolated evaluations under fixed 5G physical-layer configurations, making it difficult to perform sound comparisons, for example, to identify differences between frequency bands or channel bandwidths w.r.t. jamming resilience. To fill this gap, we implement and release an open-source 5G jammer within the ns-3 simulator. Leveraging the controllable determinism of simulation, we compare the impact of cellular jamming across different dimensions such as cellular technologies, frequencies, subcarrier spacing, and channel bandwidth. Our results show that while cellular technology and subcarrier spacing have little impact, channel bandwidth and frequency range play a critical role in improving jamming resilience.

</details>


### [166] [On fair and realistic performance evaluations for graph-based lateral movement detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29390)
*Corentin Larroche*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调研了横向移动检测研究中两个流行数据集在预处理和标注方法上的差异，发现不同论文采用的方法显著影响评估的公平性和真实性，并提出了有据可依的预处理与标注策略，重新评估三个经典方法后发现结果与原文有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向移动检测研究虽取得显著进展，但不同论文在数据集预处理和标注方式上差异巨大，导致下游评估的公平性和真实性问题，亟需系统性地审视并规范这些实践。

Method: 调研两个流行数据集的预处理和标注方法论，分析其对评估公平性和真实性的影响；提出有据可依的预处理与标注策略；在此新策略下重新评估三个广泛引用的横向移动检测方法。

Result: 重新评估三个经典横向移动检测方法后，得到的结果与原始论文报告存在显著差异，进一步凸显了数据集预处理和标注实践在检测器评估中的关键重要性。

Conclusion: 数据集预处理和标注策略对横向移动检测器的评估结果有决定性影响，研究社区需要采用标准化的预处理与标注规范，以确保评估的公平性和可比性。

Abstract: Research on lateral movement detection has made significant progress in recent years, spurred by the widespread availability of benchmark datasets that make evaluating detectors practical. However, the exact way in which these benchmark datasets are used varies across the literature: both the preprocessing applied before feeding the data to the detector and the labeling of lateral movement-related events change substantially from one paper to another. We survey preprocessing and labeling methodologies for two popular datasets and demonstrate their impact on the fairness and realism of downstream evaluations. We also propose well-grounded preprocessing and labeling policies for these datasets. Finally, we re-evaluate three widely cited lateral movement detection methods under these new policies; our results differ significantly from those reported in the original papers, further highlighting the critical importance of dataset preprocessing and labeling practices in evaluating lateral movement detectors.

</details>


### [167] [Enforcing Cryptographic Distributed-VCS Access Control with No Trust on Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29417)
*Xin Xu,Zhen Yang,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Liangqin Ren,Bo Chen,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DVAC，一种基于属性加密（ABE）和属性签名（ABS）的分布式版本控制系统访问控制方案，无需中心服务器即可实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制，并通过Ethereum智能合约实现访问策略的分布式存储与可信管理，已与Git集成并验证了较低的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCS访问控制方案依赖中心服务器，无法在完全分布式场景中部署，且缺乏对写权限的细粒度访问控制，难以满足分布式协作环境中的安全需求。

Method: DVAC基于属性加密（ABE）和属性签名（ABS）构建密码学访问控制协议，在分布式用户节点上实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制；引入版本控制适应协议保障DVCS核心功能完整性；利用Ethereum智能合约维护访问控制策略，实现分布式存储与可信管理；架构设计兼容现有DVCS（如Git），仅需最小修改。

Result: 实现了DVAC原型并集成到Git中，经过全面的性能评估，证明DVAC引入的开销是可接受的、适度的。

Conclusion: DVAC成功解决了分布式版本控制系统中无中心服务器场景下的细粒度访问控制问题，在保证数据安全的同时保持了较低的系统开销，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage changes to software code and various types of documents. Unlike CVCS, where entities obtain data from a central server, each entity in DVCS stores the entire repository and shares it independently. In VCS, existing access control schemes require the participation of a central server and cannot be deployed in a completely distributed scenario. Additionally, these schemes often fail to enforce fine-grained access control for write permissions, which is crucial for collaborative work in a distributed environment. In this paper, we propose a distributed version control system access control scheme (named DVAC), which enforces cryptographic access control on distributed user nodes based on attribute-based encryption (ABE) and attribute-based signature (ABS). DVAC is designed to enforce a cryptographic access control protocol for DVCS, which enables file granularity read and write separation access control without the support of a central server. To ensure the integrity of the core version control functions in DVCS while protecting data security, DVAC incorporates a version control adaptation protocol. Additionally, DVAC leverages Ethereum smart contracts to maintain access control policies, ensuring distributed storage and trusted management of access policies. The architecture of DVAC is designed to seamlessly integrate with existing mature DVCS, such as Git, with minimal modifications. We have implemented a prototype of DVAC and integrated it with Git. A comprehensive performance evaluation was conducted to assess the overhead introduced by DVAC, and it was demonstrated that the overhead is modest.

</details>


### [168] [Bending the Curve: Operational Cyber Epidemiology for Ransomware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29444)
*Stephen V Flowerday,Nikolay Lipskiy,Steven Furnell,Callum E Flowerday,John Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个操作性的网络流行病学框架，将SEIR传染病模型适配到勒索软件事件管理，把"暴露"定义为潜伏妥协和暂存阶段，"传染"定义为主动横向传播，并基于ISO 5477:2023和UNDRR-ISC危害信息档案定义了可互操作的勒索软件病例定义及关键信息要素，为安全运营中心提供R0和Re等决策辅助指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件通常被视为检测问题，但最具破坏性的事件更像疫情爆发——单一立足点可通过身份、管理工具和共享服务扩散，而响应者在可见性不完整的情况下需做出时间关键决策。现有方法缺乏将技术遥测与遏制决策相连的共享语言。

Method: 将SEIR流行病学模型适配到勒索软件事件管理；基于ISO 5477:2023公共卫生应急准备与响应信息管理指南和2025年UNDRR-ISC危害信息档案定义病例定义；将传播状态与观察状态分离以避免混淆扩散动态与检测能力；推导保护阈值启发式方法以将Re降至1以下；提供工具无关的剧本卡片链接操作信息与行动触发。

Result: 通过WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars等公开报道事件验证，展示了疫情式度量如何支持早期隔离、凭据遏制和恢复排序；推导了实际保护阈值启发式方法以减少Re。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提供一个共享语言，在资源约束下连接技术遥测与遏制决策，支持跨事件比较和更早的干预行动。

Abstract: Ransomware is often treated as a detection problem, but the most disruptive incidents behave more like outbreaks. A single foothold can spread through identities, administrative tools, and shared services while responders make time-critical decisions with incomplete visibility. This paper presents an operational cyber epidemiology framework that adapts the Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Removed (SEIR) model to ransomware incident management. In this ontology, Exposed denotes latent compromise and staging, including the dwell period before confirmed secondary compromise, while Infectious denotes active lateral propagation. Drawing on ISO 5477:2023 guidance for public health emergency preparedness and response information management and the 2025 UNDRR-ISC Hazard Information Profiles, the framework defines interoperable ransomware case definitions and Essential Elements of Information for cross-incident comparison. Basic and effective reproduction numbers, R0 and Re, are used as directional, near-real-time decision aids for security operations centers. Propagation state is separated from observation status to avoid confusing spread dynamics with detection capability. Publicly reported incidents, including WannaCry, NotPetya, SolarWinds, and MGM and Caesars, illustrate how outbreak-style measures can support earlier isolation, credential containment, and restoration sequencing. The paper also derives practical protection-threshold heuristics aimed at reducing Re below 1 and provides a tool-agnostic playbook card linking operational information to explicit action triggers. The primary contribution is a shared language that connects technical telemetry to containment decisions under resource constraints.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Resilience: Antifragility in Critical Infrastructure Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29550)
*Stephen Flowerday,Mauricio Papa,Ethan Flowerday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向关键基础设施网络安全的反脆弱性理论（AFT），基于五态弹性系统模型和Jensen增益等数学定义，结合CISSM事件数据库与HAI硬件在环数据集进行了双层实证验证，确立了未来反脆弱性测试的两个前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施网络安全需要超越"事后恢复"、转向"受限条件下的改进提升"的理论框架，但针对运营技术的经验性理论基础仍然匮乏。

Method: 构建五态弹性系统模型与有界数学定义（Jensen增益与事后增益）；采用双层实证设计：CISSM网络事件数据库中CI相关子集与HAI硬件在环工业控制数据集；检验三个确认性假设和一个探索性命题。

Result: OT相邻部门中破坏性或混合事件的占比显著更高（65.3%对46.8%，p<0.001）；HAI中攻击标记观测超过基线偏差第95百分位的可能性是正常观测的7.43倍（p<0.001）；连续攻击窗口间过程状态偏差显著下降（Spearman rho=-0.688，p=0.007）。

Conclusion: 研究发现确立了未来反脆弱性测试的两个前提条件：差异化的脆弱性负担和过程层面的扰动可观测性，而非直接证明自适应增益的存在。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure cybersecurity increasingly requires frameworks that move beyond recovery toward bounded improvement under disruption, yet empirically grounded theories for operational technology remain limited. This paper develops a Theory of Antifragility (AFT) for critical infrastructure (CI) cybersecurity, anchored in a five-state Resilient System Model and a bounded mathematical definition based on Jensen gain and post-disruption gain. A two-layer empirical design pairs a CI-relevant subset of the CISSM Cyber Events Database with the HAI hardware-in-the-loop industrial control dataset and tests three confirmatory hypotheses and one exploratory proposition. OT-adjacent sectors show significantly higher shares of disruptive or mixed events than comparison sectors (65.3 percent versus 46.8 percent, p less than 0.001), together with a greater concentration of physical-attack and data-attack subtypes. In HAI, attack-labeled observations were 7.43 times more likely than normal observations to exceed the 95th percentile of baseline deviation (p less than 0.001). Across successive attack windows, mean process-state deviation declined significantly (Spearman rho = -0.688, p = 0.007), indicating measurable response variation rather than proof of adaptive gain. Together, the findings establish two prerequisites for future antifragility testing: differentiated fragility burden and process-level perturbation observability.

</details>


### [170] [CWEEP: A Lexical Static Analysis Framework for CWE Early Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29604)
*Bryan Kwan,Benjamin Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CWEEP，一个用于寄存器传输级（RTL）代码安全弱点检测的静态分析框架，无需详细的安全规格即可在早期开发阶段使用，并能精确定位漏洞位置及提供自动代码修复建议。在两个SoC设计和3874个有缺陷模块的评估中，CWEEP的正确警告率高达60.8%，远优于此前工作的17.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件层成为攻击者关注焦点，硬件安全验证技术的重要性日益凸显。然而，现有的安全验证技术需要大量具备安全专业知识的人员投入，且缺乏标准方法在RTL代码中定位故障位置，这促使了CWEEP框架的开发。

Method: CWEEP是一个静态分析框架，核心特点是：不需要详细的安全规格说明，可在RTL开发早期（属性仍在构建中）使用；能够精确识别RTL中潜在漏洞的具体位置；在适用情况下支持自动代码修复建议。

Result: 在文献数据集上，CWEEP对两个SoC设计（含手动插入的漏洞）以及由大语言模型生成的3874个有缺陷模块进行了评估。CWEEP的正确警告率达到60.8%，而先前工作的工具在同一数据集上的正确警告率仅为17.5%。

Conclusion: CWEEP在硬件安全静态分析方面显著优于此前的方法，其无需详细安全规格、精确定位漏洞和自动修复建议的能力使其适合在RTL开发的早期阶段部署，有助于在硬件设计初期提升安全性。

Abstract: As the hardware layer becomes a focus point for attackers, the need for improved hardware security verification techniques is more important than ever. State-of-the-art security verification techniques require significant manual effort from individuals with security expertise. Furthermore, there is no standard method to locate where the fault lies within the register transfer level (RTL) code. This paper presents CWEEP, a static analysis framework for detecting security weaknesses in RTL. CWEEP does not require a detailed security specification, so it can be used in the early stages of RTL development while properties are still under construction. Furthermore, CWEEP can identify the exact location in the RTL where the potential vulnerability resides and supports automatic code repair suggestions when applicable. Using datasets from the literature, we evaluate the performance of CWEEP on a set of two SoC designs with manually inserted bugs and on a large language model generated dataset, consisting of 3874 buggy modules. We find that CWEEP issues a correct warning up to 60.8% of the time. In contrast, the tool from a previous work issued a correct warning 17.5% of the time for the same dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [CyberNeuro: A Privacy-Preserving Agentic Workbench for Cohort-Scale Neuroimage and Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28841)
*Ran Ren,Junhong Tong,Yunxi Kong,Yiyao Chen,Yucheng Li,Kunhao Zhou,Shaoqi Wang,Yuxiang Tao,Shuheng Cao,Zhihao Fan,Marissa DiPiero,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CyberNeuro是一个基于多智能体LLM的自动化神经影像分析工作台，配备本地部署的WandaMind模型，通过四个专用代理（Planner、Validator、Dispatcher、Reporter）实现自然语言驱动的复杂工作流执行，兼顾临床级数据隐私。在NeuroBench基准上，领域外准确率从40%提升至69%，并将token使用量大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经影像学工作流在元数据管理、流程执行、质量控制和数据管理方面需要大量人工投入，尤其对人力与算力有限的实验室构成严重障碍，迫切需要可扩展、低成本的自动化分析平台来民主化高级神经影像分析。

Method: 构建CyberNeuro代理工作台，核心包含定制的本地LLM模型WandaMind，由四个专用代理（Planner、Validator、Dispatcher、Reporter）通过安全的MCP桥接和固定执行层进行协作通信，支持自然语言驱动的复杂工作流执行，同时集成人工验证面板以确保生物医学质量控制。

Result: 在NeuroBench公开基准上，领域外准确率从基线模型的40%提升至69%；在10批次端到端队列工作流中，WandaMind本地配置对比Neuroclaw，token使用量约为10.6%（本地）和61.7%（云服务提供商）。

Conclusion: CyberNeuro展示了多智能体LLM技术在神经影像分析自动化方面的巨大潜力，显著提升了分析准确性和资源效率，同时通过本地部署保障数据隐私，为资源有限的实验室提供了可行的先进神经影像分析解决方案。

Abstract: Despite tremendous success in neuroimaging methodology, making large-scale, high-dimensional datasets ready for AI/ML applications remains a critical operational bottleneck. Conventional workflows require extensive manual effort across metadata curation, pipeline execution, post-processing quality control, and data management, a burden that disproportionately excludes laboratories with limited manpower and computational infrastructure. To address this real-world barrier, there is an urgent need for scalable, cost-effective computational platforms that democratize advanced neuroimaging analytics and accelerate discoveries in mental health and clinical translation. Capitalizing on multi-agent LLM breakthroughs, we introduce CyberNeuro, an agentic workbench with a tailored local LLM-model ('WandaMind') for automated neuroimaging and health-data analysis. Driven by four dedicated agents (Planner, Validator, Dispatcher, and Reporter) communicating via a secure MCP bridge and a pinned execution layer, CyberNeuro enables researchers to execute complex workflows using natural language while maintaining clinical-grade data privacy. On the public NeuroBench suite, CyberNeuro increases held-out domain accuracy from 40% to 69% over the baseline model. Beyond automated metrics, the platform integrates a human-in-the-loop verification panel to ensure rigorous biomedical quality control. Across the same end-to-end 10-batch cohort workflow suite, the local WandaMind configuration completed all tasks with an estimated aggregate token count of about 10.6% using WandaMind and 61.7% using cloud providers of token usage, compared to Neuroclaw, respectively. The platform and its production-ready modules are available at https://wanda-cyberbench.com.

</details>


### [172] [TransMem: Transforming Hidden States into Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29032)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Pinlong Cai,Botian Shi,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: TransMem是一个轻量级的推理时参数化记忆模块，通过将冻结LLM主干的历史隐藏状态转换为可复用的记忆表征，并利用门控网络动态干预当前隐状态，在不重复编码前文的情况下提升长上下文LLM代理的推理性能。在LoCoMo、HotpotQA和MemoryAgentBench上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在长交互历史中推理时，之前计算过的表征中编码的有用信息在后续生成过程中往往未被充分利用，导致任务相关证据难以被有效识别和利用。

Method: TransMem包含两个核心设计：(1) 轻量级门控网络将稀疏历史隐藏状态转化为可复用的记忆表征，并动态对当前隐藏状态施加潜在干预；(2) 证据条件自蒸馏（evidence-conditioned self-distillation），让记忆增强的学生模型处理完整上下文，并与仅使用证据的教师模型（共享同一冻结主干）的预测分布进行匹配，从而学习可迁移的记忆利用能力而非任务特定知识。

Result: 在LoCoMo上提升11.58-29.25 F1，在HotpotQA上提升10.20-13.03 F1，在MemoryAgentBench上将平均准确率从29.54%提升至40.00%，且在不同模型架构和规模上表现一致。

Conclusion: 稀疏历史隐藏状态是一种有效且高效的长上下文LLM代理记忆载体，TransMem证明了利用历史表征可以显著提升代理推理性能，且无需重新编码前文内容。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over long interaction histories, where effective reasoning requires identifying and exploiting task-relevant evidence distributed across past observations and actions. However, useful information encoded in previously computed representations is often underutilized during subsequent generation. We propose \textbf{TransMem}, a lightweight inference-time parametric memory module that transforms sparse historical hidden states from a frozen LLM backbone into reusable memory representations. TransMem uses a lightweight gating network to dynamically apply the latent intervention to the current hidden states, without repeatedly encoding the preceding context. To learn transferable memory utilization rather than task-specific knowledge, we introduce evidence-conditioned self-distillation. A memory-augmented student processes the full context and matches the predictive distribution of an evidence-only teacher that shares the same frozen backbone. Experiments on LoCoMo, HotpotQA, and MemoryAgentBench demonstrate consistent improvements across different model architectures and scales. TransMem yields gains of 11.58--29.25 $F_1$ on LoCoMo and 10.20--13.03 $F_1$ on HotpotQA, while improving the average MemoryAgentBench accuracy from 29.54\% to 40.00\%. These results establish sparse historical hidden states as an effective and efficient memory substrate for long-context LLM agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/Haodong-Lei-Ray/TransMem.

</details>


### [173] [SeekBrain: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Accelerating Neuroscience Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29347)
*Jiamin Wu,Peishan Xiang,Jingyang Chen,Yuqing Zhu,Yuxi Li,Ling Luo,Qihao Zheng,Jialiang Zu,Yongchao Wu,Mindong Liu,Haitao Wu,Chaofan Hu,Yijie Sun,Yuqi Hang,Yu Zhu,Shuo Li,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Wanghan Xu,Tianlei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Bo Zhang,Kai Wang,Lei Bai,Mianxin Liu,Wanli Ouyang,Jiulin Du,Chunfeng Song*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍SeekBrain，一个自主多智能体框架，通过领域层次化规划和跨模态数据分析加速神经科学研究发现。该框架从代码-论文对中动态构建分析方案库，结合智能体规划与执行引擎，按需生成假设和分析流程。在BrainArena基准上显著优于现有智能体基线，并在斑马鱼行为和小鼠决策任务等实际研究中展示了整合行为、神经和解剖数据的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经科学需要整合多尺度、多模态数据集以揭示智能的神经原理，但高度异构的数据和碎片化的分析工作流正在限制科学发现的效率。现有方法缺乏能够自动生成假设和分析流程的系统，亟需一个可扩展的工具来加速数据驱动的神经科学发现。

Method: SeekBrain采用自主多智能体框架，核心机制包括：(1)从代码-论文对中动态构建分析方案库，将专家编码的知识转化为可复用的分析配方；(2)领域层次化规划引擎，将复杂任务分解为可执行的子任务；(3)跨模态数据分析能力，支持行为、神经和解剖数据的整合分析；(4)自主假设生成与分析管线构建机制。

Result: 在专家标注的BrainArena基准上，SeekBrain在多种分析任务中大幅超越现有最先进智能体基线。实际部署中成功整合了行为、神经和解剖数据，揭示了斑马鱼幼虫行为的结构化分布式神经表征，以及小鼠决策任务中跨脑区解码强度的共享轴。

Conclusion: SeekBrain被确立为可扩展且实用的工具，能够有效加速神经科学中的数据驱动发现，为解决多模态数据整合和分析挑战提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Modern neuroscience relies on integrating multi-scale, multimodal datasets to uncover the neural principles underlying intelligence. However, analytical challenges posed by highly heterogeneous data and fragmented workflows increasingly constrain discoveries. Here we introduce SeekBrain, an autonomous multi-agent framework designed to accelerate neuroscience discovery through domain-grounded hierarchical planning and cross-modal data analysis. SeekBrain dynamically constructs a repertoire of analysis recipes extracted from code-paper pairs. By coupling this codified expertise with agentic planning and execution engines, the framework scalably generates hypotheses and analytical pipelines on demand. Systematic evaluation on the expert-annotated BrainArena benchmark demonstrates that SeekBrain substantially outperforms state-of-the-art agent baselines across various analysis tasks. Crucially, when deployed in real-world research, SeekBrain integrated behavioral, neural, and anatomical data to reveal structured, distributed neural representations of larval zebrafish behavior and a shared axis of regional decoding strength across the brain in a mouse decision-making task. These results establish SeekBrain as a scalable and practical tool for accelerating data-driven discoveries in neuroscience.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了满足时空逻辑STL-GO约束的多智能体路径规划问题，提出了基于混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT）的两种编码方法，并在多无人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用中广泛存在（如多机器人灭火、工厂无人机巡检），其难点在于同时处理时空约束（何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互）。现有框架虽能通过时空逻辑表达这些约束，但如何在STL-GO框架下高效规划满足约束的多智能体路径仍是一个挑战，尤其是需要编码多个可能随时间变化的图结构。

Method: 本文提出了两种编码方案：一种基于混合整数规划（MIP），另一种基于可满足性模理论（SMT），并提供了统一接口来指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范。两种方法均具有可靠性保证（soundness guarantees），可在统一的接口框架下直接比较。

Result: 在包含多无人机的搜索救援基准任务上进行了评估，对团队规模和图复杂度进行了消融实验，展示了所提编码在动态多图交互场景下的表达能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的MIP和SMT两种编码均能有效解决STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划问题，验证了编码方法在不同图复杂度和团队规模下的适用性与表现力。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述综合257篇论文，提出一个涵盖行为、安全、时序、监管和多智能体五个维度的分类框架，用于刻画智能体AI系统的验证问题。分析表明行为评估相对成熟，而时序有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体系统保证仍不完善，最终提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统通过多步轨迹运行，其行为取决于决策如何随时间展开并适应环境变化，现有组件测试和一次性输入-输出评估方法已不足以验证这类系统的可接受行为，需要系统性地刻画智能体系统的验证问题。

Method: 通过综合257篇论文（涵盖智能体评估、软件保证、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指南），构建五维分类法（行为、安全、时序、监管、多智能体），利用该分类法映射现有方法并识别覆盖缺口，同时通过医疗、工业运营和智能出行三个跨领域案例研究进行操作性说明。

Result: 行为评估相对成熟，但时序有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体系统保证仍发展不足；三个安全关键领域的案例研究展示了五个维度的重复出现模式及文献中记录的失败模式。

Conclusion: 可信部署智能体AI系统依赖于在上下文中验证轨迹而非仅评估孤立组件，核心研究议程应聚焦于有界自主规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和可审计证据结构。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [176] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向Agentic AI的分层架构，并从反应式LLM接口演进到具有记忆、规划和持续执行的持久性自主AI代理。通过对OpenClaw-Ollama全栈系统的原型验证，证明持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从反应式LLM向持久性、可执行系统的快速转变暴露了Agentic AI架构理解上的关键空白，特别是在推理、编排和执行层的分离方面。现有的统一框架仍然有限，亟需一个用于设计和评估全栈代理系统的统一架构。

Method: 提出全面的分层Agentic AI架构，将OpenClaw和Ollama作为全栈系统进行集成分析——Ollama作为LLM推理层，OpenClaw作为代理运行时编排层，整合推理、工具使用和动作执行，并进行原型实验验证。

Result: 实验验证表明，持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非独立模型，且架构复杂度越高，系统性能持续改善。

Conclusion: 该工作为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI代理的有效性，并提供了构建可扩展、安全和可信代理系统的路线图，同时指出了可扩展多代理架构、分布式自主系统和人类感知框架等未来方向。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [177] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [178] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段猜想发现流水线，包括基于局部证据模块的区域搜索、反思性验证（基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性）以及Lean 4/Mathlib中的形式化验证，目标是发现具有高"数学品味"的重大猜想。实验表明20个候选全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，且没有重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍然高度依赖专家直觉，目前缺乏一种统一的方法来系统地生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索——从显式局部证据模块出发；(2) 反思性验证——评估基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性；(3) 形式化验证——在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化检验。

Result: 在20个候选猜想上，全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，全部未被exact?直接吸收，全部未被aesop自动证明，且无显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够从自然语言稳定过渡到形式化验证，所生成的猜想具有较好的新颖性和非平凡性，有望为人类数学研究提供持久帮助。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [179] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkReset框架，该框架认为在受限上下文窗口中，长链式推理的核心瓶颈在于缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史信息。ThinkReset通过接口写回和重置机制，显式构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后的继续求解成功率，在多个长时程推理基准上稳定提升了固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式推理（long chain-of-thought）虽然能提升复杂问题的性能，但会引入冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定等问题。作者认为在受限上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈不是轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史并支持继续求解。此外，最终答案奖励驱动的长链强化学习存在关键失败模式：当模型在窗口即将耗尽前未解决任务时，最终奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: ThinkReset通过文本空间实现可复用中间接口：一方面通过接口写回（interface writeback）显式构建可复用的中间表示，另一方面通过重置（reset）机制清理上下文空间；同时直接优化重置后继续求解的成功率，以克服最终答案奖励导致的过早猜测问题。

Result: 在多个长时程推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口条件下一致地提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: ThinkReset验证了"可复用中间接口"这一视角的有效性，表明在上下文窗口受限情况下，构建可迁移的中间接口比轨迹压缩或测试时控制更为关键，为解决长链推理的窗口瓶颈提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [180] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter），一种利用强化学习（GRPO）训练的任务感知提示词重写器，能够将用户提示词自动改写为任务优化的提示词，从而提升下游LLM的性能，在问答、摘要、算术推理等任务上取得了稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）通常需要精心设计的提示词才能发挥全部潜力，这对非专家用户构成了使用障碍。现有方法未能充分解决自动生成任务优化提示词的问题。

Method: 提出TAPR模型，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行强化学习训练，奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对改写后提示词及任务输出的联合评估。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为TAPR的基座模型进行微调。

Result: 在问答、摘要、算术推理等多种任务上，TAPR相比基座模型在提示词重写能力上取得一致提升。在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准测试上取得更高准确率，生成的提示词包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言。

Conclusion: TAPR通过任务感知的提示词自动重写，有效降低了LLM使用门槛，提升了非专家用户的提示词质量，进而改善下游任务性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [181] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GenCDSR框架，通过跨域混合分词机制和跨域串并行解码策略，解决跨域序列推荐中生成式推荐的两大问题（忽略跨域协同相关性、解码效率低），在三个公开数据集上平均准确率提升1.5%、推理延迟降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐（CDSR）的生成式推荐方法存在两个关键问题：（1）分词过程中忽略了跨域的协同相关性；（2）采用如beam search等低效解码策略，阻碍实时部署。

Method: （1）设计跨域混合分词机制，采用多塔架构，通过层级共享-特异和细粒度码本联合捕获跨域共性与领域特异性；（2）开发跨域串并行解码策略，利用层级SID结构实现部分并行化生成，降低推理延迟的同时保持生成一致性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，与最先进基线相比，GenCDSR平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%。

Conclusion: GenCDSR有效解决了CDSR中生成式推荐忽略跨域协同相关性和解码效率低的问题，在准确性和推理效率上均取得显著提升，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation.
